FAQ

What's the highest scoring 10-letter word? RAZZMATAZZ at 48 Scrabble points.

How many 10-letter words are there? 20302 words are valid in standard word-game dictionaries.

Back to WordCrack

Are these words valid in Scrabble and Words With Friends? Yes — this list uses a standard tournament word dictionary accepted by both games.
